# Agents · R · part 5

1,197 entries, README to README. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **README**: One directory per agent. Everything here is config-as-code: hand-edited, diffed in git, hot-reloaded without a restart. ([nouverse/nouride](https://github.com/nouverse/nouride/blob/HEAD/content/agents/README.md) · ★113)
- **README**: Claude Code shows every agent's name and description to the main model in every session, so this folder holds only a small **core** set. All other specialists live as **agent skills** (`skills/agent-&lt;name&gt;/`): skills that run as their own subagent (`context: fork`) when invoked, listed by name only until then. Nothing was removed; see `INDEX.md` or the `toolkit-router` skill to find one. ([travisjneuman/.claude](https://github.com/travisjneuman/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/README.md) · ★113)

- **README**: Every agent lives in a collective directory that includes an `AGENTS.md` marker and an `agents/` folder. If an agent sits at the workspace root (`.genie/agents/review.md`) it keeps the simple id `review`. - **README**: Single-responsibility agents for building features in FuzeInfra. Each agent owns **one domain**, hard-stops at its boundary, and is **forbidden from grading the whole feature** — it reports only its own slice. This exists because a monolithic feature-agent once reported "DONE/GREEN" while siblings (UI/tests) were unbuilt. Scoped agents that report an honest, scoped "done" are the guardrail.
